def objective_fft(a):
    a = np.asarray(a,float)
    n = a.size
    s = a.sum()
    if s < 1e-12: return inf
    a = a/s
    # full convolution via fft
    N = 1
    while N < 2*n-1: N <<=1
    F = np.fft.rfft(a, N)
    C = np.fft.irfft(F*F, N).real[:2*n-1]
    M = C.max()
    return 2*n*M
    # also maybe c sum =1

We can cache? not needed.

def lse_loss_grad(a, beta):
    n=a.size; s=a.sum(); a=a/s
    N... C = ...
    # ensure positive (c >=0) maybe small negative from FFT; clip 0
    # Use shifted for stability: m = max(c); loss = m + log(sum(exp(beta*(c-m))))
    # weights w = beta * c * exp(t) / sum? Derivative wrt a of loss:
    # L = log∑exp(β c_k). ∂L/∂a_i = ∑ (exp(βc_k)/Z) * β ∂c_k/∂a_i.
    # If c_k=0, exp maybe e^{0}=1 for βc=0; but product? exp(βc) finite. Fine.
    t = beta * (C - C.max())
    E = np.exp(t)
    Z = E.sum()
    w = beta * C * E / Z
    # Need grad_i = ∑_k w_k a_{k-i}. This is conv(w, a_reverse) length 3n-2 slice n-1..2n-2?
    N2=1 while N2 < (2n-1)+n-1 =3n-2
    Fw = rfft(w,N2); Fa = rfft(a[::-1],N2); G = irfft(Fw*Fa,N2)[n-1:2*n-1]
    return loss, G

function optimize_sequence(a0, n, max_time, beta_range, seed):
    a = a0.clone normalized sum=1
    loga = log(a+eps)
    mom1=mom2 zeros
    t=0
    for iteration while time:
       C, M = fft_conv(a)
       # smooth loss: use beta = beta0 + growth * it maybe
       # weights for active lags:
       u = C / max(M,1e-300)
       # To emphasize large but smooth: w = exp(beta*(u-1))
       w = exp(beta*(u-1))  # active=1, inactive smaller
       # Maybe multiply by C? For entropy derivative, use w*C? If u active near1 and if C small? For active C=M. Use w *= C? Since ∂LSE(C)/∂a has factor C. If using LSE(u), derivative also ~ (β/M)* C e... so factor C. Use w_exp = beta * C * exp(beta*(u-1))? The exp handles, w=C*exp(beta*(u-1)). We'll use weights = C * exp(beta * (u-1)) (or beta omitted). Normalize weights sum to 1? For gradient magnitude, conv weights maybe O(1).
       G = conv(weights, a_rev)
       # Since sum weights maybe? We can subtract mean? For log update, use G - G.mean() to enforce scale invariance.
       grad = G - G.mean()
       adam step
       normalize a
       record best M
    return best

Pseudo:
def power_optimize(a, max_iters, p_start=2, p_end=64, min_percent=...):
    a = normalize
    loga = np.log(a)
    best = a; best_obj
    G = None
    m = v = 0
    for it in range:
        p = p_start + (p_end-p_start)*(it/max_iters)^? maybe exponential.
        C = conv_fft(a)
        M = C.max()
        if M==0: break
        w = (C / M)**(p-1)
        # Optional: if p large, clip min to avoid zeros? Keep positive.
        # To prevent using only exact max, add smoothing: w = 0.01 + 0.99*(C/M)^{p-1}
        w = alpha + (1-alpha)*(C/M)**(p-1) maybe.
        G = conv(w, a_rev)
        grad = G - (a@G)
        # Maybe scale grad to unit L2
        grad_norm = np.sqrt(np.mean(grad*grad))
        if grad_norm < 1e-14: break
        step = lr * grad / grad_norm? If using exponent, step unitless. lr maybe 0.2.
        loga += -lr * grad / grad_norm
        a = exp(loga); a/=a.sum()
        # Objective with current a

Convolution FFT:
def fft_convolution(a):
    n = a.size
    N = 1 << (2*n-2).bit_length()  # maybe use next power of two for rfft; n=0 handle
    F = np.fft.rfft(a, N)
    return np.fft.irfft(F*F, N).real[:2*n-1]

This may produce tiny negative; clip.

Objective = 2*n*C.max() / S^2. We'll normalize a sum=1 before so 2nM. For returning list, no normalization needed, evaluate handles. But keep normalized for objective.

Convolution for gradient:
def weighted_conv(a, w):
    n=a.size; L=2*n-1
    N = 1 << ((3*n-2).bit_length())? Since length w =2n-1, a_rev=n. convolution length 3n-2. Use next pow2.
    Fw=np.fft.rfft(w,N); Frev=np.fft.rfft(a[::-1],N)
    conv=np.fft.irfft(Fw*Frev,N).real
    return conv[n-1:2*n-1]  # length n

Check slice indices: conv length N real, first 3n-2 valid. n-1..2n-2 inclusive length n. If n=1, conv[n-1:2*n-1] = conv[0:1] ok.

Could w have NaN? clip.

Power optimize function details:
def optimize_power(a0, max_time, n_transform, p0, p1, lr0, lr1, eps=1e-12):
    a = np.asarray(a0,float).copy()
    a = np.clip(a,0,None)
    s=a.sum()
    if s<=0: a=np.ones(n); s=n
    a/=s
    n=a.size
    loga=np.log(a+1e-300)
    best=a.copy(); best_obj=objective_internal(a)
    # Use Adam? Let's implement simple normalized gradient descent with momentum.
    m = np.zeros(n); v = np.zeros(n)
    beta1=0.9; beta2=0.999; eps=1e-8
    # Since gradient magnitude after normalization may not be scale-free for Adam? Use raw grad.
    for t in range(max_iters/time budget):
        C=fft_conv(a)
        M=C.max()
        if M <=0: break
        # Update p schedule: p = p0 + (p1-p0)*progress; maybe p = p0 * exp? Use progress = elapsed/budget.
        p = p0 + (p1-p0)*progress
        # weights:
        ratio = C / M
        ratio = np.maximum(ratio, 1e-12)
        # p may >1; pow
        w = np.power(ratio, p-1)
        # Add small constant to prevent zero for low lags (help full search) e.g. 0.001
        w = 0.002 + w
        # G
        G=weighted_conv(a,w)
        # Normalize grad to fixed scale
        # For minimizing ∑ w C on simplex, grad = G - a@G.
        offset = np.dot(a,G)
        grad = G - offset
        # Use Adam step size lr = lr0*(lr1/lr0)^progress maybe.
        lr = lr0 * (lr1/lr0)**progress
        m = (1-beta1)*m + beta1*grad
        v = (1-beta2)*v + beta2*grad*grad
        mhat = m/(1-beta1**(t+1)); vhat=v/(1-beta2**(t+1))
        step = -lr * mhat/(np.sqrt(vhat)+1e-8)
        # Reduce effective step using max absolute to avoid huge log swings?
        # Since mhat/sqrt(vhat) approx O(1), lr controls.
        loga = loga + step
        # Normalize a
        # To avoid under/overflow, subtract max loga
        loga -= np.max(loga)
        a = np.exp(loga)
        a /= a.sum()
        # clip tiny negative? exp positive.
        obj = 2*n*M  # M from before update? Use new objective after update maybe expensive? We can compute quickly? Need M new for best. We can compute new C in next loop. For simplicity use previous M? But best after update should use new. Could recompute with same C? a changed. Use cheap upper? Recompute at each iteration maybe doubles FFT. Instead compute new C next iteration and update best then. For efficiency, compute objective at start of loop using C from previous iteration? Set a_prev. Let's compute objective after update with fft_conv again? That's two conv per iter. Might be okay n~4000, 2400s maybe many less. But we can update best with previous C? If a changed, not exact. Better compute new C at the beginning of next loop and update best. At end, recompute.
        # Maybe every 5 iterations or when improvement.
    after loop compute final objective.

    # Try α values [alpha_high, 0.5alpha, 0.125...] and accept best objective (using FFT) maybe expensive. Without line search, use small.

def random_smooth_init(n,rng):
    x=np.linspace(0, 2*np.pi, n, endpoint=False)
    base=np.ones(n)
    for k in range(1,7):
        amp=rng.uniform(0,0.25) maybe total <0.8; phase=rng.uniform(0,2pi)
        base += amp*np.cos(k*x+phase)
    # maybe add another sine sign with low amp
    a=np.clip(base,1e-9,None)
    return normalize

def initial_geometric(n, logr):
   i=np.arange(n)
   x=logr * i/(n-1) # total log ratio
   a=np.exp(x); a/=a.sum()
   # ensure nonneg
```

```
def smooth_optimize(a0, max_seconds, tag=''):
    a=normalize
    best=a; best_obj=internal_score(a)
    n=a.size
    start=time.monotonic()
    deadline=start+max_seconds
    p_start=2.0; p_end=256.0
    lr_start=0.5; lr_end=0.05
    nonimp=0
    rng = np.random.default_rng(int(np.sum(np.abs(a))*1e6)+abs(hash(tag))%1000? hash strings random. Better use global? We can seed with n and time? Use `np.random.default_rng(int(time.time()*1000)%2**32)`? time not random. Use fixed seed plus n.
```
No closures. Need get rng top-level? We can create inside.

Iteration:
```
while time.monotonic() < deadline:
    elapsed = time.monotonic()-start
    progress = min(1, elapsed/max_seconds)
    p = p_start * (p_end/p_start)**progress
    lr = lr_start * (lr_end/lr_start)**progress
    C=fft_conv(a)
    if C.size==0: break
    M=C.max()
    current_obj = 2*n*M
    if current_obj < best_obj - 1e-12: best=...
    if M <= 0: break
    ratio = np.clip(C / M, 1e-12, 1.0)
    w = 0.001 + np.power(ratio, p-1.0)
    G=weighted_conv(a,w)
    offset=np.dot(a,G)
    d=G-offset
    norm=np.sqrt(np.mean(d*d))
    if not finite or norm<1e-15: nonimp +=1; if nonimp>30: break; continue
    d=d/norm
    accepted=False
    # Try factors descending
    for factor in (2.0,1.0,0.5,0.25,0.125):
        delta=lr*factor
        if delta > 1.0: delta=1.0
        a_trial=a*np.exp(-delta*d)  # Wait minimize, direction d=grad, update log -= delta d. If d high in center, reduce center. yes.
        a_trial = a_trial / a_trial.sum()
        obj = internal_score(a_trial)
        if obj < current_obj - 1e-12:
            a=a_trial; current_obj=obj; accepted=True; break
    if accepted:
       nonimp=0
       if current_obj < best_obj: best...
    else:
       # try random small step
       if time left > 1:
          step = 0.3*(rng.random(n)-0.5)
          # maybe smooth random: convolve with small kernel? Use frequency? Simple random not smooth; but for escape.
          step = 0.1 * step
          a_trial=normalize(a*np.exp(step)); obj=internal_score(a_trial)
          if obj < current_obj: accept
          else:
              nonimp +=1
              if nonimp>40: break
```
Sign check: We are minimizing P; gradient G = ∂P/∂a. In log variables δlog = -d where d=G-offset. So a_trial=a*exp(-delta*d). Good.
But if current_obj computed from normalized a. Good.
Use internal_score for trial does another normalization; okay.

Potential issue: d normalized by mean square; if d mostly small, gradient signal. Good.

def random_smooth_initial(n,rng):
    x=np.arange(n, dtype=float)
    base=np.ones(n)
    xnorm = x/ max(1,n-1)
    # trend
    base += rng.uniform(-0.5,0.5)* (2*xnorm-1)
    for m in range(1,6):
        amp = rng.uniform(0,0.12)  # total max 0.6
        ph = rng.uniform(0,2*np.pi)
        base += amp*np.cos(2*np.pi*m*xnorm+ph)
        base += rng.uniform(-amp,amp)*np.sin(...?) maybe use ph.
    base = np.clip(base, 1e-12, None)
    return normalize_nonnegative(base)
```

Now `smooth_optimize`. Let's add parameters: `p_start=2.0, p_end=256.0, lr_start=0.4, lr_end=0.05, max_seconds`. It returns best sequence.
Pseudo detailed:
```
def smooth_optimize(a0, max_seconds, tag='', p_start=2.0, p_end=256.0, lr_start=0.4, lr_end=0.05):
    a = normalize_nonnegative(a0)
    n=a.size
    if n<=0: return a
    deadline = time.monotonic()+max_seconds
    best=a.copy(); best_obj=internal_score(a)
    rng = np.random.default_rng(12345+n+hash(tag)%100000) # hash string? Python hash random per run? PYTHONHASHSEED random, not deterministic but okay. Better seed = (n*1000003 + int.from_bytes(tag.encode(), 'little')?)
```
Hash of string not stable; but seed not critical. Could use `seed = 12345 + n + len(tag)*7919`. For different tags distinct? Use simple.
```
    elapsed0=time.monotonic()
    nonimp=0
    it=0
    while time.monotonic() < deadline:
       elapsed = min(max_seconds, time.monotonic()-start)
       progress = elapsed/max_seconds if max_seconds>0 else 1
       p = p_start * (p_end/p_start)**progress
       lr = lr_start * (lr_end/lr_start)**progress
       C=fft_convolution(a)
       M=C.max()
       if not finite or M<=0: break
       current=2*n*M
       if current < best_obj -1e-12:
          best=a.copy(); best_obj=current; nonimp=0
       else:
          # no immediate improvement from previous step? Actually current is objective of a at start of iter, best updated each iter after previous. If no improvement currently relative best, maybe count? But best maybe from earlier with higher a? We'll update after accepted.
       if M <= 1e-300: break
       ratio = C / M
       ratio = np.clip(ratio, 1e-12, 1.0)
       w = 0.001 + np.power(ratio, p-1.0)
       # If p large, w range 0.001-1
       G=weighted_convolution(a,w)
       offset=np.dot(a,G)
       d=G-offset
       norm=np.linalg.norm(d) # Could use sqrt mean. Use L2, step scale independent of n? If L2 grows sqrt(n), direction magnitude. We want per-coordinate std; use sqrt(mean). Use that.
       if not finite: break
       if norm < 1e-14:
          # try random
       d=d/norm
       accepted=False
       for factor in (2.0,1.0,0.5,0.25,0.125):
          delta=lr*factor
          if delta > 1.0: delta=1.0
          a_trial = a * np.exp(-delta*d)
          a_trial = normalize_nonnegative(a_trial)
          obj_trial = internal_score(a_trial)
          if obj_trial < current - 1e-12:
             a=a_trial; current=obj_trial; accepted=True; break
          # if time left low break? no
       if accepted:
          if current < best_obj -1e-12:
             best=a.copy(); best_obj=current
          nonimp=0
       else:
          # Try random asymmetric smoothed step
          # Generate random smooth direction: random frequencies
          d_rand = np.zeros(n)
          for m in range(1,4):
             amp=rng.uniform(-1,1); ph=rng.uniform(0,2*np.pi); d_rand += amp*np.cos(2πm*i/n+ph)
          a_trial = normalize_nonnegative(a * np.exp(0.1*d_rand))
          obj_trial = internal_score(a_trial)
          if obj_trial < current - 1e-12: accept
          else:
             nonimp +=1
             if nonimp > 60: break
       it +=1
       # If time less than 0.5 break? loop condition.
    # final exact? no
    return best
```
Potential issue: `d` computed for current a. If accepted trial using d from current, then next loop recomputes. Good.
`norm` should be sqrt(mean(d^2)), not L2. Use `num = float(np.sqrt(np.mean(d*d)))`.
`d_rand` should be smoothed, but random cos can be low frequency. Good. Add also linear trend random.

But line search factors: current objective from C of current a. If current a is best? At each trial, `internal_score` recomputes with trial normalization. If objective improves by tiny, accept. If no improvement due numerical, nonimp. At start of loop best update if current < best_obj (could happen after random accepted not updated? We update inside; okay).

Step direction sign: Suppose uniform, d high center, d positive. exp(-delta*d) reduces center. Good.

What if d has negative in some places, exp(-delta*d) increases. Good.

   start=int(rng.integers(0,n))
   stop=min(n,start+width)
   candidate=best.copy()

   score=internal_score

Let's write `score_candidates`:
```
def score_candidates(candidates, k=3):
    out=[]
    for idx,seq in enumerate(candidates):
       if seq is None: continue
       a=normalize_nonnegative(seq)
       if a.size==0: continue
       sc=internal_score(a)
       if np.isfinite(sc): out.append((sc,a,idx))
    out.sort(key=lambda x:x[0])
    res=[]
    for sc,a,idx in out[:k]: res.append(a)
    return res
```
No lambda! The rule says don't use lambda functions. `out.sort(key=...)` with lambda not allowed? It says no lambda functions. We must avoid. Use `out.sort()` because tuple (score,a,idx) sorting compares score then array elements if tie, might error comparing arrays? Python cannot compare arrays in sort if first tie? If scores tie, it tries compare arrays -> error. Need avoid lambda. We can sort by indices:
```

```
We can implement custom sort or use `sorted(range(len(out)), key=out.__getitem__)`? key can be method? `key=out.__getitem__` not lambda but still method; but it returns tuple score,a. Tie array compare issue. We can include a unique sequence number as first? No. Use `out.sort()` if we store (score, idx) only, then map. Since idx unique, tuple (float, int), no lambda. Let's do:

Let's write `next_power_of_two`:
```
def next_power_of_two(m):
    m = int(max(1, m))
    p = 1
    while p < m:
        p <<= 1
    return p
```
Avoid bit_length? fine.

`normalize_nonnegative`:
```
def normalize_nonnegative(a):
    if a is None: return None
    arr=np.asarray(a, dtype=np.float64)
    if arr.ndim !=1: arr=arr.ravel()
    if arr.size==0: return None
    arr=np.where(np.isfinite(arr), arr, 0.0)
    arr=np.clip(arr,0.0,None)
    s=float(np.sum(arr))
    if s <= 1e-15:
        return np.ones(arr.size, dtype=np.float64)/float(arr.size)
    return arr/s
```
If arr size 1 and value 0, returns 1. Good. But if size 0? returns None. In callers handle.

`fft_convolution`:
```
def fft_convolution(a):
    a=normalize_nonnegative(a)
    if a is None: return np.array([])
    n=a.size
    if n==0: return np.array([])
    N=next_power_of_two(2*n-1)
    F=np.fft.rfft(a, N)
    c=np.fft.irfft(F*F, N).real
    c=c[:2*n-1]
    c=np.maximum(c,0.0)
    return c
```
`internal_score`:
```
def internal_score(a):
    a=normalize_nonnegative(a)
    if a is None: return float('inf')
    n=a.size
    if n==0: return inf
    c=fft_convolution(a)
    if c.size==0: return inf
    return float(2.0*n*c.max())
```
Since normalize sum=1.

`weighted_convolution`: expects normalized a? We can not normalize? It will use a passed normalized. w length 2n-1. Do:
```
def weighted_convolution(a,w):
    n=a.size
    N=next_power_of_two(3*n-2)
    Fw=np.fft.rfft(w, N)
    Frev=np.fft.rfft(a[::-1], N)
    conv=np.fft.irfft(Fw*Frev, N).real
    out=conv[n-1:2*n-1]
    return np.maximum(out,0.0)
```
A might not be normalized but is. If n=1, N=1, slice [0:1] ok.

`symmetrize_sequence`:
```
def symmetrize_sequence(a):
    a=normalize_nonnegative(a)
    if a is None or a.size<2: return a
    return normalize_nonnegative(0.5*(a+a[::-1]))
```

`resample_sequence`:
```
def resample_sequence(a, n_target):
    a=normalize_nonnegative(a)
    if a is None: return None
    if a.size == n_target: return a.copy()
    if a.size <2:
        val=float(a[0]); return np.full(n_target, val/max(1,n_target))? But normalized val=1, output ones/n_target.
```
If a size 1 and normalized [1], resample to n_target should be uniform? Linear interpolation of single point? Use uniform. For non-normalized? normalized sum=1 => [1]. Uniform length target. Good.
```
    x_old=np.linspace(0.0,1.0,a.size)
    x_new=np.linspace(0.0,1.0,n_target)
    new=np.interp(x_new,x_old,a)
    return normalize_nonnegative(np.clip(new,0,None))
```

`trig_initial`:
```
def trig_initial(n, m, phase, amp, offset=0.0):
    if n<=0: return None
    x=(np.arange(n, dtype=float)+float(offset))/float(n)
    val=1.0 + float(amp)*np.cos(2.0*np.pi*float(m)*x + float(phase))
    # maybe small trend to avoid pure symmetric? phase covers.
    return normalize_nonnegative(np.maximum(val,1e-12))
```

`geometric_initial`:
```
def geometric_initial(n, logr):
   if n<=0: return None
   x=np.arange(n, dtype=float)
   if n>1: x = (logr * x)/float(n-1)
   else: x=np.zeros(n)
   a=np.exp(np.clip(x,-30,30))
   return normalize_nonnegative(a)
```

`linear_initial`:
```
def linear_initial(n, slope):
   x=np.linspace(0,1,max(1,n))
   val=1.0 + float(slope)*(2*x-1)
   return normalize_nonnegative(np.maximum(val,1e-12))
```

`random_smooth_initial`:
```
def random_smooth_initial(n, rng):
   x=np.linspace(0,1,max(1,n))
   val=1.0
   val = val + rng.uniform(-0.6,0.6)*(2*x-1)
   for m in range(1,7):
      amp=rng.uniform(0.0,0.08)
      ph=rng.uniform(0.0,2*np.pi)
      val = val + amp*np.cos(2π m x+ph)
   return normalize_nonnegative(np.maximum(val,1e-12))
```
Sum amp max 0.48 + trend .6 could go negative; clip. Good.

`make_initials`:
```
def make_initials(n, rng, external_best, given_seq):
    cand=[]
    if n<=0: return cand
    cand.append(np.ones(n))
    if external_best is not None:
       cand.append(resample_sequence(external_best,n))
       cand.append(symmetrize_sequence(resample_sequence(external_best,n)))
    if given_seq is not None and given_seq.size>0:
       cand.append(resample_sequence(given_seq,n))
    # geometric
    for logr in (-2.0,-1.0,-0.5,-0.25,0.25,0.5,1.0,2.0):
       cand.append(geometric_initial(n,logr))
    # linear
    for slope in (-1.0,-0.6,-0.3,0.3,0.6,1.0):
       cand.append(linear_initial(n,slope))
    # trig grid
    if n <= 2000:
       ms=[1,2,3]; phases=[0.0, np.pi/2.0, np.pi, 3*np.pi/2.0]; amps=[0.3,0.7,1.0]; offsets=[0.0,0.5]
    else:
       ms=[1,2]; phases=[0.0, np.pi/2.0, np.pi, 3*np.pi/2.0]; amps=[0.6,1.0]; offsets=[0.0,0.5]
    for offset in offsets:
      for m in ms:
        for phase in phases:
          for amp in amps:
             cand.append(trig_initial(n,m,phase,amp,offset))
    # random
    for _ in range(6):
       cand.append(random_smooth_initial(n,rng))
    # Remove None (though normalize returns None rarely)
    out=[]
    for c in cand:
       if c is not None and c.size >0:
          out.append(c)
    return out
```
Trig phase `np.pi` and offset symmetric variants may duplicate; okay.

`score_candidates` as above.

`smooth_optimize` with d1/d2.
Detailed code:
```
def smooth_optimize(a0, max_seconds, tag='', p_start=2.0, p_end=256.0, lr_start=0.6, lr_end=0.05):
    if max_seconds is None or max_seconds <= 0.1:
       return normalize_nonnegative(a0)
    a=normalize_nonnegative(a0)
    if a is None: return None
    n=a.size
    start=time.monotonic(); deadline=start+float(max_seconds)
    best=a.copy(); best_obj=internal_score(a)
    rng_seed = 987654321 + 1000003*(n % 1000003) + 7919*(len(tag) % 1000)
    rng=np.random.default_rng(rng_seed)
    nonimp=0; it=0
    print("optimize", tag, "n", n, "start obj", best_obj)
    while time.monotonic() < deadline:
       elapsed=time.monotonic()-start
       progress=max(0.0, min(1.0, elapsed/float(max_seconds)))
       p = p_start * (p_end/p_start)**progress
       lr = lr_start * (lr_end/lr_start)**progress
       C=fft_convolution(a)
       if C.size==0: break
       M=float(C.max())
       if not np.isfinite(M) or M <= 0: break
       current=2.0*n*M
       if current < best_obj - 1e-12:
          best=a.copy(); best_obj=current
       ratio=C/M
       ratio=np.clip(ratio,1e-12,1.0)
       w=0.001 + np.power(ratio, p-1.0)
       G=weighted_convolution(a,w)
       # direction gradient
       dirs=[]
       off=float(np.dot(a,G))
       d=G-off
       num=float(np.sqrt(np.mean(d*d)))
       if np.isfinite(num) and num>1e-14:
          dirs.append(d/num)
       Gpos=np.maximum(G,1e-12)
       lg=np.log(Gpos)
       offl=float(np.dot(a,lg))
       dl=lg-offl
       numl=float(np.sqrt(np.mean(dl*dl)))
       if np.isfinite(numl) and numl>1e-14:
          dirs.append(dl/numl)
       accepted=False
       if not dirs:
          # random step
          ...
       else:
          for darr in dirs:
             for factor in (1.0,0.5,0.25,0.125):
                delta=lr*factor
                if delta > 1.0: delta=1.0
                with np.errstate(over='ignore', under='ignore'):
                    aval=np.exp(-delta*darr)
                a_trial=normalize_nonnegative(a*aval)
                if a_trial is None: continue
                obj_trial=internal_score(a_trial)
                if np.isfinite(obj_trial) and obj_trial < current - 1e-12:
                   a=a_trial; current=obj_trial; accepted=True
                   if current < best_obj -1e-12:
                      best=a.copy(); best_obj=current
                   break
             if accepted: break
       if not accepted:
          # random smooth step
          x=np.linspace(0,1,n)
          dr = (2*x-1)*rng.uniform(-1,1)
          for m in (1,2,3):
              dr = dr + rng.uniform(-1,1)*np.cos(2*np.pi*m*x + rng.uniform(0,2*np.pi))
          with np.errstate(...): aval=np.exp(0.2*dr)
          a_trial=normalize_nonnegative(a*aval)
          if a_trial is not None:
             obj_trial=internal_score(a_trial)
             if np.isfinite(obj_trial) and obj_trial < current -1e-12:
                a=a_trial; current=obj_trial; accepted=True; if current<best...
       if not accepted:
          nonimp +=1
          if nonimp > 80: break
       else:
          nonimp=0
       it +=1
       if it % 200 ==0:
          print("  ", tag, "t", elapsed, "obj", current, "best", best_obj)
    return best
```

def run_stage(n, max_seconds, holder, rng, given):
    if n <=0 or max_seconds <=10: return holder
    cands=make_initials(n, rng, holder[0], given)

       a=normalize_nonnegative(s)
       if a is None or a.size != n? resampled size n; if not, continue.

    scored.sort()

    if k>=3: budgets=[0.6,0.3,0.1]
    elif k==2: budgets=[0.7,0.3]
    else: budgets=[1.0]
    for i in range(k):

def propose_candidate(seed=42, budget_s=2400, **kwargs):
   total_start=time.monotonic()
   eff = max(60.0, float(budget_s))
   deadline = total_start + eff - 5.0
   try: given = np.asarray(height_sequence_1, dtype=np.float64)
   except Exception: given=None
   if given is not None:
       given=np.where(np.isfinite(given),given,0.0)
       given=np.clip(given,0,None)
       if given.ndim !=1 or given.size ==0: given=None
   rng=np.random.default_rng(seed)
   holder=[None, float('inf')]
   holder=update_best_holder(given, holder)
   # plan
   plan=[(256,0.015),(512,0.03),(1024,0.07),(2048,0.18),(4096,0.27),(4806,0.35),(8192,0.085)] # sum 1.0
   # if given length 4806 okay. If given length maybe 2048, add stage with it? but already.
   for n, frac in plan:
       max_s = eff*frac
       if max_s < 20: max_s = min(20, max(0, deadline-time.monotonic()))
       remaining=deadline-time.monotonic()
       if remaining <= 10: break
       max_s=min(max_s, max(0, remaining-5))
       if max_s >= 10:
           holder=run_stage(n, max_s, holder, rng, given, deadline)
           print(f"after stage n={n} best={holder[1]}")
   # final polish
   remaining=deadline-time.monotonic()
   if holder[0] is not None and remaining > 60:
       pol = holder[0]
       pol_budget = min(remaining - 5, 600.0)  # not too long
       if pol_budget > 30:
           pol = local_polish(pol, pol_budget, tag='polish')
           holder=update_best_holder(pol, holder)
   # final sym/combinations
   if holder[0] is not None:
       a=holder[0]
       holder=update_best_holder(symmetrize_sequence(a), holder)
       s=symmetrize_sequence(a)
       for t in (0.25,0.5,0.75):
           cand = (1.0-t)*a + t*s
           holder=update_best_holder(cand, holder)
   # optionally exact check
   if holder[0] is not None:
       try:
           exact = evaluate_sequence([float(x) for x in holder[0]])
           if np.isfinite(exact) and exact < holder[1]:
               holder[1]=float(exact)
       except Exception:
           pass
       print("final score internal", holder[1], "len", holder[0].size)
   if holder[0] is None:
       fallback = np.full(1024, 1.0/1024.0)
       holder=[fallback, internal_score(fallback)]
   return [float(x) for x in holder[0]]
```

def local_polish(a0, max_seconds, tag=''):
    a=normalize_nonnegative(a0)
    if a is None or max_seconds<=1: return a
    n=a.size
    best=a.copy(); best_score=internal_score(a)
    rng=np.random.default_rng(555 + n + len(tag))
    start=time.monotonic(); deadline=start+max_seconds
    nonimp=0
    x=np.linspace(0,1,n)
    while time<deadline:
       cand=best.copy()
       mode=int(rng.integers(0,3))
       if mode==0:
          width=int(max(2, rng.integers(max(2,n//200), max(3,n//10))))
          starti=int(rng.integers(0,n-width+1))
          cand[starti:starti+width] *= np.exp(rng.normal(0,0.25))
       elif mode==1:
          slope=rng.uniform(-0.2,0.2)
          cand *= np.exp(slope*(2*x-1))
       else:
          ph=rng.uniform(0,2*np.pi); m=int(rng.integers(1,4)); amp=rng.uniform(0.02,0.15)
          cand *= np.exp(amp*np.cos(2π m x+ph))
       if rng.random()<0.25:
          cand=0.5*(cand+cand[::-1])
       cand=normalize_nonnegative(cand)
       sc=internal_score(cand)
       if sc < best_score -1e-12:
          best=cand; best_score=sc; nonimp=0
       else:
          nonimp +=1
          if nonimp > 5000: break
    return best
```

Potential bug in weighted_conv: The gradient G_i = sum_k w_k a_{k-i}. We use conv(w, a_rev) slice n-1:2n-1. Let's re-verify with small n=3. a=[a0,a1,a2], w=[w0,w1,w2]. conv(w,a_rev=[a2,a1,a0]) length5: index0 w0a2;1 w0a1+w1a2;2 w0a0+w1a1+w2a2;3 w1a0+w2a1;4 w2a0. Need G_i = ∑_k w_k a_{k-i} for 0<=k-i<n.
i=0: k=0 a0*w0, k=1 a1*w1, k=2 a2*w2 => w0a0+w1a1+w2a2 = index2 (n-1=2). i=1: k=1 a0*w1 + k=2 a1*w2 = index3. i=2: k=2 a0*w2 = index4. Slice 2:5 gives correct. Good.

Could w be length 2n-1? yes. For p weights, w[k]= (C_k/M)^(p-1). C_k is convolution coefficient at lag k. c_0=a0^2 etc. Gradient formula correct for c_k = ∑_{i:0≤k-i<n} a_i a_{k-i}. Derivative w.r.t a_i is a_{k-i}. Good.

`local_polish` code:
```
def local_polish(a0, max_seconds, tag=''):
    a = normalize_nonnegative(a0)
    if a is None:
        return None
    if max_seconds is None or max_seconds <= 1.0:
        return a
    n = a.size
    best = a.copy()
    best_score = score_assuming_normalized(best)
    deadline = time.monotonic() + float(max_seconds)
    rng = np.random.default_rng(98765 + n + 17*len(tag))
    x = np.linspace(0.0,1.0,n)
    nonimp=0; it=0
    while time.monotonic() < deadline:
        cand = best.copy()
        mode = int(rng.integers(0, 3))
        if mode == 0:
            lo = max(2, int(n * 0.005))
            hi = max(lo + 1, int(n * 0.05))
            width = int(rng.integers(lo, hi + 1))
            if width >= n:
                width = max(1, n // 2)
            starti = int(rng.integers(0, n - width + 1))
            cand[starti:starti+width] *= np.exp(rng.normal(0.0, 0.25))
        elif mode == 1:
            slope = float(rng.normal(0.0, 0.15))
            cand *= np.exp(slope * (2.0*x - 1.0))
        else:
            m = int(rng.integers(1, 4))
            ph = float(rng.uniform(0.0, 2.0*np.pi))
            amp = float(rng.uniform(0.05, 0.2))
            cand *= np.exp(amp * np.cos(2.0*np.pi*float(m)*x + ph))
        if rng.random() < 0.2:
            cand = 0.5 * (cand + cand[::-1])
        cand = normalize_nonnegative(cand)
        if cand is None:
            continue
        sc = score_assuming_normalized(cand)
        if np.isfinite(sc) and sc < best_score - 1e-12:
            best = cand
            best_score = sc
            nonimp = 0
        else:
            nonimp += 1
            if nonimp > 2000:
                break
        it += 1
        if it % 2000 == 0:
            print("  polish", tag, "t", time.monotonic()- (deadline-max_seconds), "score", best_score)
    return best
```

def score_assuming_normalized(a):
    if a is None: return inf
    n=a.size
    if n==0: return inf
    c=fft_convolution_raw(a)

    return float(2.0*n*m)

Let's code `run_stage` with stage_end.

```
def run_stage(n, max_seconds, holder, rng, given, deadline):
    if n<=0 or max_seconds <=0: return holder
    stage_start=time.monotonic()
    stage_end=min(stage_start+float(max_seconds), deadline)
    cands=make_initials(...)
    ...
    score_budget=min(30.0, 0.25*max_seconds)
    score_end=min(stage_start+score_budget, stage_end-10)
    for ... if time.monotonic() > score_end and len(scored)>0: break
    ...
    scored.sort()
    top=[]...
    fractions ...
    for i in range(len(top)):
       rem=stage_end-time.monotonic()
       if rem <= 10: break
       alloc=min(max_seconds*fractions[i], rem-2.0)
       if alloc < 5: break
       optimized=smooth_optimize(top[i], alloc, tag=f... )
       holder=update_best_holder(optimized, holder)
    rem=stage_end-time.monotonic()
    if rem > 30 and holder[0] is not None:
       extra=min(rem-2, max(20.0, 0.30*max_seconds))
       optimized=smooth_optimize(holder[0], extra, tag=f"stage{n}best", p_start=8.0, p_end=512.0)
       holder=update_best_holder(optimized, holder)
    print("end stage", n, "best", holder[1], "len", holder[0].size if holder[0] is not None else -1)
    return holder
```
Need if stage_end much less than stage_start+max_seconds because deadline near; okay. The `fractions` use max_seconds not remaining; if stage_end truncated by deadline, alloc min remaining. Good.

```python
"""
Search for non-negative coefficients minimizing

    2 * n * max_k conv(a, a)_k / (sum(a))^2

by normalized FFT-based power/soft-max multiplicative optimization.

Main ingredients:
  - internal FFT score and gradient (no direct O(n^2) calls during search)
  - multi-resolution starts: 256 -> 512 -> 1024 -> 2048 -> 4096 -> 4806 -> 8192
  - initialized from uniform, geometric, linear, trigonometric, random-smooth,
    the external best seed, and the supplied height_sequence_1 if available
  - line-searched log-domain updates driven by soft-max/entropy weights
  - final refinement and local polishing
"""

import time
import numpy as np


def next_power_of_two(m):
    m = int(max(1, m))
    p = 1
    while p < m:
        p <<= 1
    return p


def normalize_nonnegative(a):
    if a is None:
        return None
    arr = np.asarray(a, dtype=np.float64)
    if arr.ndim != 1:
        arr = arr.ravel()
    if arr.size == 0:
        return None
    arr = np.where(np.isfinite(arr), arr, 0.0)
    arr = np.clip(arr, 0.0, None)
    s = float(np.sum(arr))
    if s <= 1e-15:
        return np.ones(arr.size, dtype=np.float64) / float(arr.size)
    return arr / s


def fft_convolution_raw(a):
    n = a.size
    if n == 0:
        return np.array([], dtype=np.float64)
    N = next_power_of_two(2 * n - 1)
    F = np.fft.rfft(a, N)
    c = np.fft.irfft(F * F, N).real[: 2 * n - 1]
    return np.maximum(c, 0.0)


def fft_convolution(a):
    a = normalize_nonnegative(a)
    if a is None:
        return np.array([], dtype=np.float64)
    return fft_convolution_raw(a)


def score_assuming_normalized(a):
    if a is None:
        return float("inf")
    n = a.size
    if n == 0:
        return float("inf")
    c = fft_convolution_raw(a)
    if c.size == 0:
        return float("inf")
    m = float(c.max())
    if not np.isfinite(m):
        return float("inf")
    return float(2.0 * n * m)


def score_normalized(a):
    a = normalize_nonnegative(a)
    if a is None:
        return float("inf")
    return score_assuming_normalized(a)


def weighted_convolution(a, w):
    n = a.size
    if n == 0:
        return np.array([], dtype=np.float64)
    N = next_power_of_two(3 * n - 2)
    Fw = np.fft.rfft(w, N)
    Frev = np.fft.rfft(a[::-1], N)
    conv = np.fft.irfft(Fw * Frev, N).real
    return np.maximum(conv[n - 1: 2 * n - 1], 0.0)


def symmetrize_sequence(a):
    a = normalize_nonnegative(a)
    if a is None or a.size < 2:
        return a
    return normalize_nonnegative(0.5 * (a + a[::-1]))


def resample_sequence(a, n_target):
    a = normalize_nonnegative(a)
    if a is None:
        return None
    if n_target <= 0:
        return None
    if a.size == n_target:
        return a.copy()
    if a.size < 2:
        return np.full(n_target, 1.0 / float(n_target), dtype=np.float64)
    x_old = np.linspace(0.0, 1.0, a.size)
    x_new = np.linspace(0.0, 1.0, n_target)
    new = np.interp(x_new, x_old, a)
    return normalize_nonnegative(np.clip(new, 0.0, None))


def trig_initial(n, m, phase, amp, offset=0.0):
    if n <= 0:
        return None
    x = (np.arange(n, dtype=np.float64) + float(offset)) / float(n)
    val = 1.0 + float(amp) * np.cos(2.0 * np.pi * float(m) * x + float(phase))
    return normalize_nonnegative(np.maximum(val, 1e-12))


def geometric_initial(n, logr):
    if n <= 0:
        return None
    x = np.arange(n, dtype=np.float64)
    if n > 1:
        x = (float(logr) * x) / float(n - 1)
    else:
        x = np.zeros(n, dtype=np.float64)
    a = np.exp(np.clip(x, -30.0, 30.0))
    return normalize_nonnegative(a)


def linear_initial(n, slope):
    if n <= 0:
        return None
    x = np.linspace(0.0, 1.0, max(1, n))
    val = 1.0 + float(slope) * (2.0 * x - 1.0)
    return normalize_nonnegative(np.maximum(val, 1e-12))


def random_smooth_initial(n, rng):
    if n <= 0:
        return None
    x = np.linspace(0.0, 1.0, max(1, n))
    val = 1.0
    val = val + float(rng.uniform(-0.6, 0.6)) * (2.0 * x - 1.0)
    for m in range(1, 7):
        amp = float(rng.uniform(0.0, 0.08))
        ph = float(rng.uniform(0.0, 2.0 * np.pi))
        val = val + amp * np.cos(2.0 * np.pi * float(m) * x + ph)
    return normalize_nonnegative(np.maximum(val, 1e-12))


def make_initials(n, rng, external_best, given_seq):
    cands = []
    if n <= 0:
        return cands

    cands.append(np.ones(n, dtype=np.float64))

    if external_best is not None:
        r = resample_sequence(external_best, n)
        if r is not None:
            cands.append(r)
            cands.append(symmetrize_sequence(r))

    if given_seq is not None and given_seq.size > 0:
        r = resample_sequence(given_seq, n)
        if r is not None:
            cands.append(r)

    for logr in (-2.0, -1.0, -0.5, -0.25, 0.25, 0.5, 1.0, 2.0):
        cands.append(geometric_initial(n, logr))

    for slope in (-1.0, -0.6, -0.3, 0.3, 0.6, 1.0):
        cands.append(linear_initial(n, slope))

    if n <= 2000:
        ms = [1, 2, 3]
        phases = [0.0, np.pi / 2.0, np.pi, 3.0 * np.pi / 2.0]
        amps = [0.3, 0.7, 1.0]
        offsets = [0.0, 0.5]
    else:
        ms = [1, 2]
        phases = [0.0, np.pi / 2.0, np.pi, 3.0 * np.pi / 2.0]
        amps = [0.6, 1.0]
        offsets = [0.0, 0.5]

    for offset in offsets:
        for m in ms:
            for phase in phases:
                for amp in amps:
                    cands.append(trig_initial(n, m, phase, amp, offset))

    for _ in range(6):
        cands.append(random_smooth_initial(n, rng))

    out = []
    for c in cands:
        if c is not None and c.size > 0:
            out.append(c)
    return out


def score_candidates(candidates, k=3):
    scored = []
    for idx, s in enumerate(candidates):
        a = normalize_nonnegative(s)
        if a is None or a.size == 0:
            continue
        sc = score_assuming_normalized(a)
        if np.isfinite(sc):
            scored.append((sc, idx, a))
    scored.sort()
    res = []
    for i in range(min(k, len(scored))):
        res.append(scored[i][2])
    return res


def update_best_holder(seq, holder):
    a = normalize_nonnegative(seq)
    if a is None or a.size == 0:
        return holder
    sc = score_assuming_normalized(a)
    if np.isfinite(sc) and sc < holder[1]:
        holder[1] = float(sc)
        holder[0] = a
    return holder


def smooth_optimize(a0, max_seconds, tag="", p_start=2.0, p_end=512.0,
                    lr_start=0.7, lr_end=0.06):
    a = normalize_nonnegative(a0)
    if a is None:
        return None
    if max_seconds is None or max_seconds <= 0.1:
        return a

    n = a.size
    start_time = time.monotonic()
    deadline = start_time + float(max_seconds)
    best = a.copy()
    best_score = score_assuming_normalized(best)

    seed_add = 0
    for ch in str(tag):
        seed_add += ord(ch)
    rng_seed = 987654321 + 1000003 * (n % 1000003) + 7919 * (seed_add % 1000003)
    rng = np.random.default_rng(rng_seed)

    x = np.linspace(0.0, 1.0, n)
    nonimp = 0
    it = 0

    print("optimize", tag, "n", n, "obj", best_score)

    while time.monotonic() < deadline:
        elapsed = time.monotonic() - start_time
        progress = max(0.0, min(1.0, elapsed / float(max_seconds)))
        p = float(p_start) * (float(p_end) / float(p_start)) ** progress
        lr = float(lr_start) * (float(lr_end) / float(lr_start)) ** progress

        C = fft_convolution_raw(a)
        if C.size == 0:
            break
        M = float(C.max())
        if not np.isfinite(M) or M <= 0.0:
            break

        current = float(2.0 * n * M)
        if current < best_score - 1e-12:
            best_score = current
            best = a.copy()

        ratio = C / M
        ratio = np.clip(ratio, 1e-12, 1.0)
        w = 0.001 + np.power(ratio, p - 1.0)
        G = weighted_convolution(a, w)

        dirs = []

        off = float(np.dot(a, G))
        d = G - off
        num = float(np.sqrt(np.mean(d * d)))
        if np.isfinite(num) and num > 1e-14:
            dirs.append(d / num)

        Gpos = np.maximum(G, 1e-12)
        lg = np.log(Gpos)
        offl = float(np.dot(a, lg))
        dl = lg - offl
        numl = float(np.sqrt(np.mean(dl * dl)))
        if np.isfinite(numl) and numl > 1e-14:
            dirs.append(dl / numl)

        accepted = False

        if dirs:
            for darr in dirs:
                for factor in (1.0, 0.5, 0.25, 0.125):
                    delta = lr * factor
                    if delta > 1.0:
                        delta = 1.0
                    with np.errstate(over="ignore", under="ignore"):
                        scale = np.exp(-delta * darr)
                    a_trial = normalize_nonnegative(a * scale)
                    if a_trial is None:
                        continue
                    obj_trial = score_assuming_normalized(a_trial)
                    if np.isfinite(obj_trial) and obj_trial < current - 1e-12:
                        a = a_trial
                        current = obj_trial
                        if current < best_score - 1e-12:
                            best_score = current
                            best = a.copy()
                        accepted = True
                        break
                if accepted:
                    break

        if not accepted:
            dr = (2.0 * x - 1.0) * float(rng.uniform(-1.0, 1.0))
            for m in (1, 2, 3):
                dr = dr + float(rng.uniform(-1.0, 1.0)) * np.cos(
                    2.0 * np.pi * float(m) * x + float(rng.uniform(0.0, 2.0 * np.pi))
                )
            with np.errstate(over="ignore", under="ignore"):
                scale = np.exp(0.2 * dr)
            a_trial = normalize_nonnegative(a * scale)
            if a_trial is not None:
                obj_trial = score_assuming_normalized(a_trial)
                if np.isfinite(obj_trial) and obj_trial < current - 1e-12:
                    a = a_trial
                    current = obj_trial
                    if current < best_score - 1e-12:
                        best_score = current
                        best = a.copy()
                    accepted = True

        if not accepted:
            nonimp += 1
            if nonimp > 80:
                break
        else:
            nonimp = 0

        it += 1
        if it % 500 == 0:
            print("  ", tag, "t", round(elapsed, 1),
                  "cur", round(current, 8), "best", round(best_score, 8))

    return best


def local_polish(a0, max_seconds, tag=""):
    a = normalize_nonnegative(a0)
    if a is None:
        return None
    if max_seconds is None or max_seconds <= 1.0:
        return a

    n = a.size
    best = a.copy()
    best_score = score_assuming_normalized(best)

    start_time = time.monotonic()
    deadline = start_time + float(max_seconds)
    rng = np.random.default_rng(98765 + n + 17 * len(str(tag)))
    x = np.linspace(0.0, 1.0, n)

    nonimp = 0
    it = 0

    while time.monotonic() < deadline:
        cand = best.copy()
        mode = int(rng.integers(0, 3))

        if mode == 0:
            lo = max(2, int(n * 0.005))
            hi = max(lo + 1, int(n * 0.05))
            width = int(rng.integers(lo, hi + 1))
            if width >= n:
                width = max(1, n // 2)
            starti = int(rng.integers(0, n - width + 1))
            cand[starti:starti + width] *= np.exp(float(rng.normal(0.0, 0.25)))
        elif mode == 1:
            slope = float(rng.normal(0.0, 0.15))
            cand *= np.exp(slope * (2.0 * x - 1.0))
        else:
            m = int(rng.integers(1, 4))
            ph = float(rng.uniform(0.0, 2.0 * np.pi))
            amp = float(rng.uniform(0.05, 0.2))
            cand *= np.exp(amp * np.cos(2.0 * np.pi * float(m) * x + ph))

        if rng.random() < 0.2:
            cand = 0.5 * (cand + cand[::-1])

        cand = normalize_nonnegative(cand)
        if cand is None:
            continue

        sc = score_assuming_normalized(cand)
        if np.isfinite(sc) and sc < best_score - 1e-12:
            best = cand
            best_score = sc
            nonimp = 0
        else:
            nonimp += 1
            if nonimp > 2000:
                break

        it += 1
        if it % 2000 == 0:
            print("  polish", tag, "t", round(time.monotonic() - start_time, 1),
                  "score", round(best_score, 8))

    return best


def run_stage(n, max_seconds, holder, rng, given, deadline):
    if n <= 0 or max_seconds <= 0.0:
        return holder

    stage_start = time.monotonic()
    stage_end = min(stage_start + float(max_seconds), deadline)

    cands = make_initials(n, rng, holder[0], given)
    if not cands:
        cands = [np.ones(n, dtype=np.float64)]

    score_budget = min(30.0, 0.25 * float(max_seconds))
    score_end = min(stage_start + score_budget, stage_end - 10.0)

    scored = []
    for idx, s in enumerate(cands):
        if time.monotonic() > score_end and len(scored) > 0:
            break
        a = normalize_nonnegative(s)
        if a is None or a.size != n:
            continue
        sc = score_assuming_normalized(a)
        if np.isfinite(sc):
            scored.append((sc, idx, a))

    if not scored:
        a = normalize_nonnegative(np.ones(n, dtype=np.float64))
        scored.append((score_assuming_normalized(a), 0, a))

    scored.sort()
    top = []
    for i in range(min(3, len(scored))):
        top.append(scored[i][2])

    if len(top) >= 3:
        fractions = (0.55, 0.30, 0.15)
    elif len(top) == 2:
        fractions = (0.65, 0.35)
    else:
        fractions = (1.0,)

    for i in range(len(top)):
        rem = stage_end - time.monotonic()
        if rem <= 10.0:
            break
        alloc = float(max_seconds) * fractions[i]
        alloc = min(alloc, rem - 2.0)
        if alloc < 5.0:
            break

        opt = smooth_optimize(top[i], alloc, tag="stage_n{}_i{}".format(n, i))
        holder = update_best_holder(opt, holder)

    rem = stage_end - time.monotonic()
    if rem > 30.0 and holder[0] is not None:
        extra = min(rem - 2.0, max(20.0, 0.30 * float(max_seconds)))
        opt = smooth_optimize(holder[0], extra,
                              tag="stage_n{}_best".format(n),
                              p_start=8.0, p_end=1024.0,
                              lr_start=0.3, lr_end=0.03)
        holder = update_best_holder(opt, holder)

    if holder[0] is not None:
        print("stage done n={} best={} len={}".format(n, holder[1], holder[0].size))
    else:
        print("stage done n={} best=None".format(n))

    return holder


def propose_candidate(seed=42, budget_s=2400, **kwargs):
    total_start = time.monotonic()
    eff = max(1.0, float(budget_s))
    safety = min(5.0, max(0.5, 0.02 * eff))
    deadline = total_start + eff - safety

    rng = np.random.default_rng(seed)

    given = None
    try:
        g = np.asarray(height_sequence_1, dtype=np.float64)
        if g.ndim == 1 and g.size > 0:
            g = np.where(np.isfinite(g), g, 0.0)
            g = np.clip(g, 0.0, None)
            if float(np.sum(g)) > 0.0:
                given = g
    except Exception:
        given = None

    holder = [None, float("inf")]
    holder = update_best_holder(given, holder)

    plan = [
        (256, 0.01),
        (512, 0.02),
        (1024, 0.05),
        (2048, 0.12),
        (4096, 0.20),
        (4806, 0.48),
        (8192, 0.12),
    ]

    for n, frac in plan:
        remaining = deadline - time.monotonic()
        if remaining <= 10.0:
            break
        max_s = eff * float(frac)
        max_s = min(max_s, max(0.0, remaining - 5.0))
        if max_s >= 10.0:
            holder = run_stage(n, max_s, holder, rng, given, deadline)

    if holder[0] is not None:
        remaining = deadline - time.monotonic()
        if remaining > 120.0:
            refine_budget = min(remaining - 5.0, 600.0)
            if refine_budget > 60.0:
                refined = smooth_optimize(holder[0], refine_budget,
                                          tag="final_refine",
                                          p_start=8.0, p_end=1024.0,
                                          lr_start=0.3, lr_end=0.03)
                holder = update_best_holder(refined, holder)

        remaining = deadline - time.monotonic()
        if remaining > 50.0:
            polish_budget = min(remaining - 5.0, 300.0)
            if polish_budget > 20.0:
                polished = local_polish(holder[0], polish_budget, tag="final_polish")
                holder = update_best_holder(polished, holder)

    if holder[0] is not None:
        a = holder[0]
        holder = update_best_holder(symmetrize_sequence(a), holder)
        s = symmetrize_sequence(a)
        if s is not None:
            for t in (0.25, 0.5, 0.75):
                cand = (1.0 - t) * a + t * s
                holder = update_best_holder(cand, holder)
        holder = update_best_holder(a, holder)

    if holder[0] is None:
        fallback_n = 1024
        holder = [np.full(fallback_n, 1.0 / float(fallback_n), dtype=np.float64),
                  score_normalized(np.full(fallback_n, 1.0 / float(fallback_n), dtype=np.float64))]

    print("final internal score", holder[1], "length", holder[0].size)
    return [float(x) for x in holder[0]]
```
